- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo, defaults to 10m) - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. "check the deploy every 5 minutes", "keep running /babysit-prs"). 69	/** 内部 item id → 原版 item id（无映射 -1） */
70	export function vanillaIdOfItem(itemId: number): number {

72	  if (!def) return -1;
73	  if (def.vid !== undefined) return def.vid;

88	  if (forDecrafting) {

90	    if (d !== undefined && d !== -1) return d;
91	  }
92	  const c = ITEM_COUNTS_AS[vid];
93	  if (c !== undefined && c !== -1) return c;

97	/** 音乐盒动态分支（ShimmerTransforms.cs:95-105：createTile==139 按 placeStyle）：
98	 *  90→5538 / 89→5579 / 97→5638 / 96→5639 / 其余→576（钢琴） */
99	const MUSIC_BOX_TRANSFORM: Record<number, number> = { 90: 5538, 89: 5579, 97: 5638, 96: 5639 };

101	/** 月相砖动态分支（ShimmerTransforms.cs:108-110 + GetLunarBrickTransformFromMoonPhase
102	 *  :113-125;MoonPhase 枚举序 = Main.moonPhase 0-7:Terraaria.Enums/MoonPhase.cs）:
103	 *  Full→5408 / TQL→5401 / HL→5403 / QL→5402 / Empty→5406 / QR→5407 / HR→5405 / TQR→5404 */
104	const LUNAR_BRICK_TRANSFORM = [5408, 5401, 5403, 5402, 5406, 5407, 5405, 5404];

106	/** ShimmerTransforms.GetTransformToItem（:88-111）：表优先；音乐盒按 placeStyle；
107	 *  3461 月相砖按当前月相（运行时由调用方传 world.clock.moonPhase） */
108	export function getTransformToItem(vid: number, moonPhase?: number): number {
109	  const t = ITEM_TRANSFORM[vid];
110	  if (t !== undefined && t > 0) return t;
111	  if (vid === 3461 && moonPhase !== undefined) {
112	    return LUNAR_BRICK_TRANSFORM[moonPhase & 7];
113	  }
114	  const def = ITEM_DEFS[internalIdOfVanilla(vid)];
115	  if (def?.tile === 'v_139_musicboxes' && def.placeStyle !== undefined) {
116	    return MUSIC_BOX_TRANSFORM[def.placeStyle] ?? 576;
117	  }
118	  return 0;
119	}

121	/** ShimmerTransforms.IsItemTransformLocked（:33-40）：月后物品需已败月总 */
122	export function isItemTransformLocked(vid: number, downedMoonlord: boolean): boolean {
123	  return !downedMoonlord && ITEM_POST_MOONLORD.has(vid);
124	}

126	// ============ decraft（ShimmerTransforms.cs 2026-08-13 完整移植,替换 GAP） ============
127	
128	/** 解锁门（ShimmerTransforms.UpdateRecipeSets :82-86：RecipeSets.PostSkeletron =
129	 *  配方含材料 154(骨头);PostGolem = 含 1101）+ IsRecipeIndexDecraftLocked(:47-62) */
130	export function isRecipeIndexDecraftLocked(
131	  recipeIndex: number,
132	  downedBoss3: boolean,     // NPC.downedBoss3(骷髅王;本仓 flags downed_35/downedSkeletron)
133	  downedGolemBoss: boolean, // NPC.downedGolemBoss(本仓 flags downed_245)
134	): boolean {
135	  if (recipeIndex < 0) return false;
136	  const r = recipeAt(recipeIndex);
137	  if (!r) return false;
138	  if (!downedBoss3 && r.items.some((it) => it.id === 154)) return true;
139	  if (!downedGolemBoss && r.items.some((it) => it.id === 1101)) return true;
140	  return false;
141	}
142	
143	/** ShimmerTransforms.IsItemDecraftableAndIsDecraftUnlocked（:64-80）：
144	 *  等价物(forDecrafting)有配方 + 未锁 + stack ≥ createStack */
145	export function isItemDecraftableAndUnlocked(
146	  vid: number, stack: number,
147	  crimsonWorld: boolean, downedBoss3: boolean, downedGolemBoss: boolean,
148	): boolean {
149	  const eq = getShimmerEquivalentType(vid, true);
150	  const idx = getDecraftingRecipeIndex(eq, crimsonWorld);
151	  if (isRecipeIndexDecraftLocked(idx, downedBoss3, downedGolemBoss)) return false;
152	  if (idx < 0) return false;
153	  const r = recipeAt(idx)!;
154	  return Math.floor(stack / r.createStack) > 0;
155	}
156	
157	/** Item.FindDecraftAmount（Item.cs:49063-49071）：stack / createStack(向下取整) */
158	export function findDecraftAmount(vid: number, stack: number, crimsonWorld: boolean): number {
159	  const idx = getDecraftingRecipeIndex(getShimmerEquivalentType(vid, true), crimsonWorld);
160	  if (idx < 0) return -1;
161	  return Math.floor(stack / recipeAt(idx)!.createStack);
162	}

164	/** RecipeGroup.DecraftItemId（RecipeGroup.cs:59）：组内**价值最低**物品代表
165	 *  （OrderBy(value).First()——等价取组注册序首个,稳定排序）。
166	 *  ⚠️ value 表语义:value JSON 只存 SetDefaults case 内显式赋值;**缺表 = 原版
167	 *  value 0**（Item.ResetStats :48596 的重置默认）,绝非"未知"。曾误用
168	 *  MAX_SAFE_INTEGER 回退 → Wood 组(9 无显式 value=0)被 5215(显式 value)抢位,
169	 *  与原版"木配方 decraft 返还普通木材"相悖 */
170	const GROUP_DECRAFT_ID = new Map<number, number>();
171	export function groupDecraftItemId(groupId: number): number {
172	  const hit = GROUP_DECRAFT_ID.get(groupId);
173	  if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
174	  const items = RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(groupId)] ?? [];
175	  let best = items[0] ?? 0;
176	  let bestV = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
177	  for (const it of items) {
178	    const v = (itemValueJson as Record<string, number>)[String(it)] ?? 0; // 缺表=原版 0(ResetStats 默认)
179	    if (v < bestV) { bestV = v; best = it; }
180	  }
181	  GROUP_DECRAFT_ID.set(groupId, best);
182	  return best;
183	}
184	
185	/** 材料 → decraft 返还物（WorldItem.cs:1895-1897 IsRecipeGroup ? DecraftItemId : id）：
186	 *  材料命中本配方任一 acceptedGroup → 返组代表（groupDecraftItemId） */
187	function decraftItemFor(r: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof recipeAt>>, itemId: number): number {
188	  for (const gid of r.groups) {
189	    const items = RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(gid)];
190	    if (items && (items as number[]).includes(itemId)) return groupDecraftItemId(gid);
191	  }
192	  return itemId;
193	}
194	
195	export interface DecraftPile { vid: number; stack: number }
196	
197	export interface DecraftOutcome {
198	  /** 返还垛（配方材料序；单垛 ≤9999，超额拆垛 = 原版逐垛独立 Item.NewItem :1913-1921） */
199	  piles: DecraftPile[];
200	  /** 原物品消耗量（次数 × createStack，:1927） */
201	  consumed: number;
202	  /** ≥2 材料 → 掉落物按序号抛撒（requiredItem[1].stack > 0，:1882） */
203	  spread: boolean;
204	}
205	
206	/** decraft 返还计算（WorldItem.GetShimmered :1879-1934 纯函数化）：
207	 *  无配方 / 次数 0 → null。rand3 语义 = Main.rand.Next(3)，仅 alchemy 配方消费
208	 *  （逐单位 1/3 概率蒸发，:1904-1911）；注入以便测试确定性断言 */
209	export function decraftOutcome(
210	  vid: number, stack: number,
211	  crimsonWorld: boolean,
212	  rand3: () => number = () => Math.floor(Math.random() * 3),
213	): DecraftOutcome | null {
214	  const idx = getDecraftingRecipeIndex(getShimmerEquivalentType(vid, true), crimsonWorld);
215	  if (idx < 0) return null;
216	  const r = recipeAt(idx)!;
217	  const times = Math.floor(stack / r.createStack);
218	  if (times <= 0) return null;
219	  // AddCustomShimmerResult 覆盖表（:1884-1891）替代材料表
220	  const entries: Array<{ id: number; stack: number }> = r.shimmer?.length
221	    ? r.shimmer.map(([id, st]) => ({ id, stack: st }))
222	    : r.items;
223	  const piles: DecraftPile[] = [];
224	  for (const e of entries) {
225	    if (e.id <= 0 || e.stack <= 0) break;      // :1898-1901 requiredItem 空位终止
226	    let qty = times * e.stack;
227	    if (r.alchemy) {
228	      for (let u = qty; u > 0; u--) if (rand3() === 0) qty--;
229	    }
230	    const target = decraftItemFor(r, e.id);
231	    while (qty > 0) {
232	      const chunk = Math.min(qty, 9999);       // :1913-1916
233	      piles.push({ vid: target, stack: chunk });
234	      qty -= chunk;
235	    }
236	  }
237	  return { piles, consumed: times * r.createStack, spread: (r.items[1]?.stack ?? 0) > 0 };
238	}

240	/** Item.CanShimmer（Item.cs:49045-49061）全分支 1:1：
241	 *  ① 月后锁 → false（:49048）
242	 *  ② 560 史莱姆王冠且雨进行中 → false（:49052）
243	 *  ③ (:49056) 无转化目标 ∧ 不可分解 ∧ 非钱币 ∧ (非 4986 或已解锁彩虹史莱姆)
244	 *     → 只剩 makeNPC > 0 一条路（:49058）——4986 未解锁时特例放行（:49060）
245	 *  decraftCtx 缺省 = 不启用 decraft 判定（调用方旧签名兼容） */
246	export function canShimmerItem(
247	  vid: number, downedMoonlord = false, moonPhase?: number,
248	  decraftCtx?: { stack: number; crimsonWorld: boolean; downedBoss3: boolean; downedGolemBoss: boolean },
249	  shimmerCtx?: { slimeRain?: boolean; rainbowSlimeUnlocked?: boolean },
250	): boolean {
251	  if (isItemTransformLocked(vid, downedMoonlord)) return false;
252	  if (vid === 560 && shimmerCtx?.slimeRain) return false;
253	  const special4986 = vid === 4986 && !shimmerCtx?.rainbowSlimeUnlocked; // :49056 反例
254	  if (!special4986
255	    && getTransformToItem(vid, moonPhase) <= 0
256	    && !COMMON_COIN.has(vid)
257	    && !(decraftCtx && isItemDecraftableAndUnlocked(vid, decraftCtx.stack, decraftCtx.crimsonWorld, decraftCtx.downedBoss3, decraftCtx.downedGolemBoss))) {
258	    return makeNpcOfItem(vid) > 0;
259	  }
260	  return true;
261	}

263	/** Item.makeNPC（Item.cs:47613-47626 DefaultToCapturedCritter）：瓶中小动物物品 →
264	 *  放生 NPC id。tools/extract-shimmer.mjs 从 DefaultToCapturedCritter 调用点提取

266	export function makeNpcOfItem(vid: number): number {

270	/** 钱币→微光化币面额放大（WorldItem.cs:1791-1810）：铜 ×1 / 银 ×100 / 金 ×10000 /
271	 *  铂 stack 钳 1 后 ×1000000。返回入 coinLuck 的铜币面额（stack 一并放大） */
272	export function coinLuckAmount(vid: number, stack: number): number {
273	  switch (vid) {
274	    case 72: return stack * 100;
275	    case 73: return stack * 10000;
276	    case 74: return Math.min(stack, 1) * 1000000;
277	    default: return stack; // 71 铜币 ×1
278	  }
279	}

303	 *  中心格微光即刻 GetShimmered，不经 buff）不受本表约束 */

308	// ============ 玩家脱困传送（ShimmerHelper.FindSpotWithoutShimmer 近似） ============
309	
310	/** TryFindingShimmerFreeSpot（Player.cs:28414-28469）净效果版：以 (tx,ty) 为心，
311	 *  半径 r ∈ {1,3,…,59}（原版 num=60 循环步 2）找第一个满足 IsSpotShimmerFree
312	 *  （ShimmerHelper.cs:67-82 三判）的落点：
313	 *  ① 玩家盒 3 格高（y-2..y）无实心无微光（SolidCollision(landing, w, h)）
314	 *  ② 下方 100px（7 格）内有地面（SolidCollision(+height, 100, allowSolidTop)——
315	 *     非紧贴一格；旧实现过严致湖底斜坡/半砖旁找不到）
316	 *  ③ 玩家盒 + 下方 100px 全无微光（WetCollision(landing, w, h+100) && shimmer → 拒）——
317	 *     旧实现漏此判：湖面之上 3 格空气 + 湖面在 7 格内的列被当有效落点 → 传送后
318	 *     落回湖里（用户实测"传送完还在湖里"）
319	 *  **三层兜底**（原版 :28442-28469）：玩家周围失败 → 世界出生点周围 →
320	 *  主出生点（本仓二者同源 spawnX/Y，合并为一层）——保证必能脱困，不会永久卡住 */
321	export function findShimmerFreeSpot(
322	  st: TileStore, tx: number, ty: number,
323	  fallbackTx?: number, fallbackTy?: number,
324	): { x: number; y: number } | null {
325	  const clear = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
326	    for (let j = y - 2; j <= y; j++) {
327	      if (!st.inBounds(x, j)) return false;
328	      const i = st.idx(x, j);
329	      if (st.isSolid(x, j) || (st.liquid[i] > 0 && st.liquidType[i] === 4)) return false;
330	    }
331	    return true;
332	  };
333	  // 原版第二判：落点下方 100px（约 7 格）内存在 solid 或 solidTop（allowSolidTop=true）
334	  const groundBelow = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
335	    for (let j = y + 1; j <= y + 7; j++) {
336	      if (!st.inBounds(x, j)) return false;
337	      if (st.isSolid(x, j) || st.isPlatform(x, j)) return true;
338	    }
339	    return false;
340	  };
341	  // 原版第三判（ShimmerHelper.cs:80-81 WetCollision(landing, w, h+100)）：落点
342	  // 下方 100px 内不得有微光——微光列（如湖面正上方的空气）视为无效落点
343	  const shimmerBelow = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
344	    for (let j = y + 1; j <= y + 7; j++) {
345	      if (!st.inBounds(x, j)) return false;
346	      const i = st.idx(x, j);
347	      if (st.liquid[i] > 0 && st.liquidType[i] === 4) return false;
348	    }
349	    return true;
350	  };
351	  const search = (cx: number, cy: number): { x: number; y: number } | null => {
352	    for (let r = 1; r < 60; r += 2) {
353	      // 切比雪夫半径逐圈外扩（原版 i 循环步 2 语义；原版仅扫环上 8 点/圈，
354	      // 此处全量整数扫描为其超集——命中的落点满足同一谓词，行为相容）
355	      for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
356	        for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
357	          if (Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) < r - 1) continue; // 只扫外环
358	          const x = cx + dx, y = cy + dy;
359	          if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || !groundBelow(x, y) || !shimmerBelow(x, y)) continue;
360	          if (clear(x, y)) return { x, y };
361	        }
362	      }
363	    }
364	    return null;
365	  };
366	  return search(tx, ty)
367	    ?? (fallbackTx !== undefined && fallbackTy !== undefined ? search(fallbackTx, fallbackTy) : null);
368	}
369	
370	/** 世界坐标落点 → 玩家传送位（左上角 px 锚，ShimmerHelper.cs:9 + Player.cs:28400-28402）。
371	 *  原版 FindSpotWithoutShimmer 返回 vector2 = (tile*16, tile*16) + (-width/2, -height)——
372	 *  即【玩家盒左上角】坐标（脚底正好贴 tile 底边）；ShimmerUnstuck 再 Teleport(vector+(0,-2))。
373	 *  旧实现 {x: tile*16, y: tile*16-2} 漏掉 -width/2/-height → 盒顶对齐格顶、脚部沉入
374	 *  下方实心 40px = 用户实测"半身在地里"。默认 20/42 = Player 构造尺寸（Player.cs:55083）。 */
375	export function shimmerTeleportPos(
376	  spot: { x: number; y: number }, w = 20, h = 42,
377	): { x: number; y: number } {
378	  return { x: spot.x * TILE - w / 2, y: spot.y * TILE - h - 2 };
379	}
380

```
	public Color GetImmuneAlpha(Color newColor, float alphaReduction)
	{
		float num = (float)(255 - immuneAlpha) / 255f;
		if (alphaReduction > 0f)
		{
			num *= 1f - alphaReduction;
		}
		if (shimmerTransparency > 0f)
		{
			if ((double)shimmerTransparency >= 0.8)
			{
				return Color.Transparent;
			}
			num *= 1f - shimmerTransparency;
			num *= 1f - shimmerTransparency;
			num *= 1f - shimmerTransparency;
		}
		if (immuneAlpha > 125)
		{
			return Color.Transparent;
		}
		return Color.Multiply(newColor, num);
	}

	public Color GetImmuneAlphaPure(Color newColor, float alphaReduction)
	{
		float num = (float)(255 - immuneAlpha) / 255f;
		if (alphaReduction > 0f)
		{
			num *= 1f - alphaReduction;
		}
		if (shimmerTransparency > 0f)
		{
			num *= 1f - shimmerTransparency;
		}
		return Color.Multiply(newColor, num);
	}

	public Color GetDeathAlpha(Color newColor)
	{
		int r = newColor.R + (int)((double)immuneAlpha * 0.9);
		int g = newColor.G + (int)((double)immuneAlpha * 0.5);
		int b = newColor.B + (int)((double)immuneAlpha * 0.5);
		int num = newColor.A + (int)((double)immuneAlpha * 0.4);
		if (num < 0)
		{
			num = 0;
		}
		if (num > 255)
		{
			num = 255;
		}
		return new Color(r, g, b, num);
	}

```

					sourceRectangle.Y = 1280;

				else

				Lighting.GetCornerColors(num3, num4, out var vertices);

				Main.DrawTileInWater(drawOffset, num3, num4);
				Main.tileBatch.Draw(_liquidTextures[num2].Value, new Vector2(num3 << 4, num4 << 4) + drawOffset + liquidOffset, sourceRectangle, vertices, Vector2.Zero, 1f, SpriteEffects.None);

36	/** GetShimmerBaseColor :803-807（float 版）：Lerp((0.647,0.510,0.933),(0.804,0.804,1), 0.1+wave×0.4) → 0-255 浮点。

83	 * Canvas2D 无顶点色，故把 16×16 tile 分 2×2 子块（8×8），每子块取四角双线性
84	 * 插值在其中心位置的色，以 multiply 叠在已画的 water_14 上（=纹理×色，同原版 modulate）。
85	 */
86	function applyShimmerBaseTint(
87	  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, x: number, y: number,
88	  dstX: number, dstY: number, w: number, h: number, tVis: number,
89	): void {
90	  const c00 = shimmerBaseColorF(x, y, tVis), c10 = shimmerBaseColorF(x + 1, y, tVis);
91	  const c01 = shimmerBaseColorF(x, y + 1, tVis), c11 = shimmerBaseColorF(x + 1, y + 1, tVis);
92	  ctx.save();
93	  // 原版 SetShimmerVertexColors 的乘法是【无条件 modulate】（纹理×顶点色），不带
94	  // 透明 pass 的 0.75 衰减——若沿用调用方残留的 globalAlpha，白色基底（表面格
95	  // Y=1280 整块纯白）只会被"部分染色"，表层色带被冲淡成灰白。故强制 1.0 全乘。
96	  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
97	  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
98	  const subW = Math.ceil(w / 2), subH = Math.ceil(h / 2);
99	  for (let by = 0; by < 2; by++) {
100	    for (let bx = 0; bx < 2; bx++) {
101	      const bw = Math.min(subW, w - bx * subW), bh = Math.min(subH, h - by * subH);
102	      if (bw <= 0 || bh <= 0) continue;
103	      // 子块中心在 tile 内的归一化位置（dstX 相对 x*16 有壁值裁剪偏移）→ 四角双线性插值
104	      const u = (dstX + bx * subW + bw / 2 - x * 16) / 16;
105	      const v = (dstY + by * subH + bh / 2 - y * 16) / 16;
106	      const ch = (i: number) => c00[i] * (1 - u) * (1 - v) + c10[i] * u * (1 - v)
107	        + c01[i] * (1 - u) * v + c11[i] * u * v;
108	      ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${Math.round(ch(0))},${Math.round(ch(1))},${Math.round(ch(2))})`;
109	      ctx.fillRect(dstX + bx * subW, dstY + by * subH, bw, bh);
110	    }
111	  }
112	  ctx.restore();

116	function hue2rgb(v1: number, v2: number, vH: number): number {
117	  if (vH < 0) vH += 1;
118	  if (vH > 1) vH -= 1;
119	  if (6 * vH < 1) return v1 + (v2 - v1) * 6 * vH;
120	  if (2 * vH < 1) return v2;
121	  if (3 * vH < 1) return v1 + (v2 - v1) * ((2 / 3) - vH) * 6;
122	  return v1;
123	}
124	/** Main.hslToRgb 1:1（GetShimmerGlitterColor :766-771 以 s=1/l=0.5 调用）→ RGB 0-1 */
125	function hslToRgb(hue: number, sat: number, lum: number): [number, number, number] {
126	  if (sat === 0) return [lum, lum, lum];
127	  const v2 = lum < 0.5 ? lum * (1 + sat) : lum + sat - lum * sat;
128	  const v1 = 2 * lum - v2;
129	  return [hue2rgb(v1, v2, hue + 1 / 3), hue2rgb(v1, v2, hue), hue2rgb(v1, v2, hue - 1 / 3)];
130	}

	private static uint SimpleWhiteNoise(uint x, uint y)

		x = 36469 * (x & 0xFFFF) + (x >> 16);

		return (x << 16) + y;

// ================= 弹幕反射管线（原版 Projectile.cs:12050-12060 + NPC.cs:67003-67059） =================

/** 可反射 aiStyle 白名单（Projectile.CanBeReflected，Projectile.cs:20224）：
 *  1 箭/子弹直飞弹、2 抛物线投掷物、8/21/24/28/29/131 各特化直线弹。
 *  另有 type 728（超级星星）/955（坠落星）无条件可反射（:20220）。 */
const REFLECTABLE_AI_STYLES = new Set([1, 2, 8, 21, 24, 28, 29, 131]);

/** 可反射弹体（调用方自报运动学字段；hostile/reflected/damage 参与门禁） */
export interface ReflectableProj {
  x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number;
  vx: number; vy: number;
  damage: number;
  hostile?: boolean;
  /** 已反射标记（本仓显式防重复；原版靠 friendly→hostile 翻转天然拦截二次反射） */
  reflected?: boolean;
}

/** 原版 Projectile.CanBeReflected（Projectile.cs:20216-20230）：
 *  active && friendly && !hostile && damage>0，且 type∈{728,955} 或
 *  aiStyle∈{1,2,8,21,24,28,29,131}。本仓 friendly 无字段——非 hostile 即友方。 */
export function projCanBeReflected(
  projId: number,
  proj: Pick<ReflectableProj, 'hostile' | 'damage'>,
): boolean {
  if (proj.hostile || !(proj.damage > 0)) return false;
  if (projId === 728 || projId === 955) return true;
  return REFLECTABLE_AI_STYLES.has(projectileData(projId)?.aiStyle ?? -1);
}

/** 原版 NPC.ReflectProjectile（NPC.cs:67036-67059）：
 *  - 音效 Item150 + 尘 31×3（velocity×0.3）；
 *  - reflected=true、friendly=false、hostile=true（owner 不变——"朝发射者"
 *    方向读 Main.player[owner]，本仓单主角恒为 game.player）；
 *  - 速度 = normalize(随机方向×原速 + 朝发射者×20) × 原速（:67047-67055，
 *    即随机散射偏向回打玩家；原速取 oldVelocity.Length，此处用当前速度近似）；
 *  - damage ÷2 两次（int 语义 ≈ ÷4）+ penetrate=1（穿透归一，穿透字段的
 *    清理由调用方按各自存储执行）。 */
export function reflectProjectile(proj: ReflectableProj, game: GameHooks): void {
  const cx = proj.x + proj.w / 2, cy = proj.y + proj.h / 2;
  // Item_150 未入音效提取库（public/sounds 无此 wav）：playSfxFile 未命中静默，
  // 回退 tink（Tink_0/1 金属弹开近似）——素材补齐后自动切真声
  if (!game.playSfxFile?.('Item_150', 1, cx, cy)) game.playSfx('tink', 0.9, cx, cy);
  game.spawnParticles(cx, cy, '#D8D8E0', 3, 0.6, { life: 24, damp: 0.9 });
  proj.reflected = true;
  proj.hostile = true;
  // 反射向量（NPC.cs:67047-67055）
  const p = game.player;
  const speed = Math.hypot(proj.vx, proj.vy);
  let ox = p.cx - cx, oy = p.cy - cy;
  const od = Math.hypot(ox, oy);
  if (od > 0.001) { ox /= od; oy /= od; }
  else if (speed > 0.001) { ox = -proj.vx / speed; oy = -proj.vy / speed; } // 重合退化:原路弹回
  else { ox = 0; oy = -1; }
  // 随机方向 = Main.rand.Next(-100,101) 双轴归一（此处 [-1,1) 均匀近似）
  const rx = Math.random() * 2 - 1, ry = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
  let dx = rx * speed + ox * 20, dy = ry * speed + oy * 20;
  const dl = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
  if (dl > 0.001) { proj.vx = (dx / dl) * speed; proj.vy = (dy / dl) * speed; }
  else { proj.vx = ox * 6; proj.vy = oy * 6; } // 零速退化:低速直飞回发射者
  // 伤害衰减（int 连除两次）+ penetrate=1（:67056-67058）
  proj.damage = Math.floor(Math.floor(proj.damage / 2) / 2);
}

/** 弹体命中敌怪分支的反射门禁 + 执行（原版 Projectile.cs:12050-12060：
 *  flag6 = targetNPC.reflectsProjectiles（getGoodWorld 星弹特例未接入——运行时
 *  无 FTW 标志），命中重叠本身即 NPC.CanReflectProjectile 的 Hitbox 相交）。
 *  返回 true = 已反射，调用方跳过本次伤害结算并结束本帧命中迭代
 *  （对应原版 keepIterating=false; return）。 */
export function tryReflectProjectile(
  proj: ReflectableProj,
  projId: number,
  e: { reflectsProjectiles?: boolean },
  game: GameHooks,
): boolean {
  if (!e.reflectsProjectiles) return false;
  if (proj.reflected) return false; // 显式防重复（原版由 hostile 翻转天然拦截）
  if (!projCanBeReflected(projId, proj)) return false;
  reflectProjectile(proj, game);
  return true;
}

 *  其余弹型的 debuff 链(毒/诅咒/灵液/暗影焰等)随对应批次逐条接入。 */

      // 经典 50% Next(180,420)t → buff 20 Poison

````
Original ReflectProjectile velocity math:
```csharp
Vector2 vector = Main.player[proj.owner].Center - proj.Center;  // to owner
vector.Normalize();
vector *= proj.oldVelocity.Length();   // unit-to-owner × oldSpeed
proj.velocity = new Vector2(rand(-100,101), rand(-100,101));
proj.velocity.Normalize();
proj.velocity *= vector.Length();      // random unit × oldSpeed
proj.velocity += vector * 20f;         // random*speed + toOwner*20
proj.velocity.Normalize();
proj.velocity *= vector.Length();      // normalize × oldSpeed
```
Result speed = oldSpeed (vector.Length()). Port: speed = hypot(vx,vy) [current speed, not oldVelocity — acknowledged approximation]; dx = rx*speed + ox*20; normalized; ×speed. ✓ Matches structure (modulo uniform vs integer-lattice random distribution). Good.

Note original reflects ALL projectiles intersecting (ReflectProjectiles called from NPC AI each frame), while the port only reflects on the projectile's hit iteration against that NPC. Minor structural difference but the visual outcome similar. Fine.

One notable thing: `proj.damage /= 2; proj.damage /= 2;` — damage is int in Terraria → integer division twice. Port uses Math.floor twice. ✓

Now the bestiary UI. Let me check BestiaryPanel.ts and data/Bestiary.ts consumption.
````

58	const GOLD_CRITTER_NORMALS: Record<number, number[]> = {
59	  443: [46], 442: [74], 592: [55], 444: [356], 601: [599], 445: [361], 446: [377],

61	};
62	/** NPCID.Sets.GoldCrittersCollection（NPCID.cs:4450）——金种全见门 */

64	
65	const HIDE = new Set(BESTIARY_HIDE_IDS);
66	const REDIRECT = BESTIARY_CREDIT_REDIRECT as Record<number, number>;

69	/** NPC.cs:96360 GetBestiaryCreditId：netID → creditId（经 ModifyNPCIds 归并） */
70	export function bestiaryCreditId(netId: number): string {
71	  return NPC_NAME_BY_ID[REDIRECT[netId] ?? netId] ?? String(netId);
72	}
73	
74	/** NPC.CountsAsACritter（NPC.cs:6803-6812）——遇数扫描/小动物条目判定 */
75	export function countsAsACritter(npcId: number): boolean {
76	  const v = VANILLA_NPCS[String(npcId)];
77	  if (!v) return false;
78	  return v.lifeMax <= 5 && v.damage === 0 && npcId !== 594 && npcId !== 686;
79	}

81	/** BestiaryEntryUnlockState（0 未知 1 仅头像 2 +属性 3 +掉落 4 +掉落率） */
82	export const enum UnlockState {
83	  NotKnown = 0,
84	  PortraitOnly = 1,
85	  Stats = 2,
86	  DropsNoRates = 3,
87	  DropsWithRates = 4,
88	}
89	
90	type Source =
91	  | { t: 'kill'; credit: string; quick: boolean; full: number }
92	  | { t: 'sight'; credit: string }
93	  | { t: 'chat'; credit: string };
94	
95	export interface BestiaryEntryDef {
96	  creditId: string;
97	  kind: 'enemy' | 'town' | 'critter';
98	  sources: Source[];
99	  /** 金小动物门：任一来源解锁后仍需全图已见至少一只金种（GoldCritterUICollectionInfoProvider） */
100	  goldGate: boolean;
101	}
102	
103	function killSource(npcId: number, quick: boolean): Source {
104	  return {
105	    t: 'kill',
106	    credit: bestiaryCreditId(npcId),
107	    quick,
108	    full: KILLS_TO_FULL[npcId] ?? BESTIARY_DEFAULT_FULL_KILLS,
109	  };
110	}
111	const sightSource = (npcId: number): Source => ({ t: 'sight', credit: bestiaryCreditId(npcId) });
112	const chatSource = (npcId: number): Source => ({ t: 'chat', credit: bestiaryCreditId(npcId) });
113	
114	/** 全量 NPC 的 boss 旗（Enemy.ts VANILLA_BOSS_IDS 同表；BestiaryEntry.Enemy :37 quickUnlock 判定） */
115	const VANILLA_BOSS_IDS = new Set([
116	  4, 13, 35, 50, 68, 113, 125, 126, 127, 134, 222, 245, 262, 266, 370, 395, 398, 439, 636, 657,
117	  325, 327, 345, 346,
118	]);
119	
120	let entryCache: BestiaryEntryDef[] | null = null;
121	
122	/** 条目注册表（BestiaryDatabaseNPCsPopulator.Populate 的数据层等价；惰性构建一次） */
123	export function bestiaryEntries(): BestiaryEntryDef[] {
124	  if (entryCache) return entryCache;
125	  const out: BestiaryEntryDef[] = [];
126	  for (const key of Object.keys(NPC_NAME_BY_ID)) {
127	    const id = Number(key);
128	    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id === 0 || HIDE.has(id)) continue;
129	    const isTown = BESTIARY_TOWN_IDS.has(id);
130	    // AddEmptyEntries_CrittersAndEnemies_Automated：isLikeATownNPC 走手工城镇表，
131	    // 其余按 CountsAsACritter 分小动物/敌怪
132	    const kind: BestiaryEntryDef['kind'] = isTown ? 'town' : countsAsACritter(id) ? 'critter' : 'enemy';
133	    let sources: Source[];
134	    let goldGate = false;
135	    const goldNormals = GOLD_CRITTER_NORMALS[id];
136	    if (goldNormals) {
137	      // 金小动物：本体遇 + 同族普通种遇；解锁态恒 0/4
138	      sources = [sightSource(id), ...goldNormals.map(sightSource)];
139	      goldGate = true;
140	    } else if (id in CRITTER_PAIR) {
141	      sources = [sightSource(id), sightSource(CRITTER_PAIR[id])];
142	    } else if (id in MULTI_SOURCES) {
143	      sources = MULTI_SOURCES[id].map((s) =>
144	        s === 'kill' ? killSource(id, isQuick(id))
145	          : s === 'chat' ? chatSource(id)
146	            : s[1] === 'kill' ? killSource(s[0], isQuick(s[0])) : chatSource(s[0]));
147	    } else if (id in KILL_CREDIT_FROM) {
148	      // 击杀记账归并族（Populator :600-616）：读母体杀数，quickUnlock=true
149	      sources = [killSource(KILL_CREDIT_FROM[id], true)];
150	    } else if (kind === 'town') {
151	      sources = [chatSource(id)];
152	    } else if (kind === 'critter') {
153	      sources = [sightSource(id)];
154	    } else {
155	      sources = [killSource(id, isQuick(id))];
156	    }
157	    out.push({ creditId: bestiaryCreditId(id), kind, sources, goldGate });
158	  }
159	  entryCache = out;
160	  return out;
161	}
162	
163	function isQuick(id: number): boolean {
164	  return VANILLA_BOSS_IDS.has(id) || COUNTED_AS_BOSS.has(id) || QUICK_UNLOCK_IDS.has(id);
165	}
166	
167	/** CommonEnemyUICollectionInfoProvider.GetUnlockStateByKillCount（:60-85） */
168	export function unlockStateByKillCount(killCount: number, quick: boolean, full: number): UnlockState {
169	  const half = Math.floor(full / 2);
170	  const fifth = Math.floor(full / 5);
171	  if (quick && killCount > 0) return UnlockState.DropsWithRates;
172	  if (killCount >= full) return UnlockState.DropsWithRates;
173	  if (killCount >= half) return UnlockState.DropsNoRates;
174	  if (killCount >= fifth) return UnlockState.Stats;
175	  if (killCount >= 1) return UnlockState.PortraitOnly;
176	  return UnlockState.NotKnown;
177	}
178	
179	export interface BestiarySave {
180	  /** 杀数（creditId → 次数） */
181	  k: Record<string, number>;
182	  /** 遇数（creditId 集） */
183	  s: string[];
184	  /** 聊天解锁（creditId 集） */
185	  c: string[];
186	}
187	
188	/**
189	 * 图鉴统计器（BestiaryUnlocksTracker 1:1）——原版是随世界文件持久化的
190	 * IPersistentPerWorldContent（WorldFile.cs:3399/3405），挂在 World 上。
191	 */
192	export class BestiaryTracker {
193	  readonly kills = new Map<string, number>();
194	  readonly sights = new Set<string>();
195	  readonly chats = new Set<string>();
196	
197	  /** NPCKillsTracker.RegisterKill（杀数上限 999999999） */
198	  registerKill(netId: number): void {
199	    const id = bestiaryCreditId(netId);
200	    const next = Math.min((this.kills.get(id) ?? 0) + 1, KILL_CAP);
201	    this.kills.set(id, next);
202	  }
203	
204	  /** NPCWasNearPlayerTracker.RegisterWasNearby（首遇置位即可，幂等） */
205	  registerSight(netId: number): void {
206	    this.sights.add(bestiaryCreditId(netId));
207	  }
208	
209	  /** NPCWasChatWithTracker.RegisterChatStartWith（幂等） */
210	  registerChat(netId: number): void {
211	    this.chats.add(bestiaryCreditId(netId));
212	  }
213	
214	  private anyGoldSeen(): boolean {
215	    for (const g of GOLD_CRITTER_IDS) {
216	      if (this.sights.has(bestiaryCreditId(g))) return true;
217	    }
218	    return false;
219	  }
220	
221	  /** 条目解锁档（各 UIInfoProvider.GetEntryUICollectionInfo 的数据层等价） */
222	  unlockState(entry: BestiaryEntryDef): UnlockState {
223	    let state = UnlockState.NotKnown;
224	    for (const s of entry.sources) {
225	      const st = s.t === 'kill'
226	        ? unlockStateByKillCount(this.kills.get(s.credit) ?? 0, s.quick, s.full)
227	        : (s.t === 'sight' ? this.sights.has(s.credit) : this.chats.has(s.credit))
228	          ? UnlockState.DropsWithRates
229	          : UnlockState.NotKnown;
230	      if (st > state) state = st;
231	    }
232	    if (entry.goldGate && state > UnlockState.NotKnown && !this.anyGoldSeen()) {
233	      // GoldCritterUICollectionInfoProvider.TryFindingOneGoldCritterThatIsAlreadyUnlocked
234	      return UnlockState.NotKnown;
235	    }
236	    return state;
237	  }
238	
239	  /** 条目是否已解锁（完成度分母口径：UnlockState > NotKnown） */
240	  isUnlocked(entry: BestiaryEntryDef): boolean {
241	    return this.unlockState(entry) > UnlockState.NotKnown;
242	  }
243	
244	  /** Main.GetBestiaryProgressReport（:65003-65020）——percent=已解锁/总数，总数 0 → 1 */
245	  completion(): { total: number; unlocked: number; percent: number } {
246	    const entries = bestiaryEntries();
247	    let unlocked = 0;
248	    for (const e of entries) if (this.isUnlocked(e)) unlocked++;
249	    return {
250	      total: entries.length,
251	      unlocked,
252	      percent: entries.length === 0 ? 1 : unlocked / entries.length,
253	    };
254	  }
255	
256	  toSave(): BestiarySave {
257	    const k: Record<string, number> = {};
258	    for (const [id, n] of this.kills) k[id] = n;
259	    return { k, s: [...this.sights], c: [...this.chats] };
260	  }
261	
262	  static fromSave(data: BestiarySave | undefined): BestiaryTracker {
263	    const t = new BestiaryTracker();
264	    if (!data) return t;
265	    if (data.k) for (const [id, n] of Object.entries(data.k)) {
266	      if (typeof n === 'number' && n > 0) t.kills.set(id, Math.min(n, KILL_CAP));
267	    }
268	    if (data.s) for (const id of data.s) t.sights.add(id);
269	    if (data.c) for (const id of data.c) t.chats.add(id);
270	    return t;
271	  }
272	
273	  /** WorldGen.cs:7068 Reset（世界重生成时清零） */
274	  reset(): void {
275	    this.kills.clear();
276	    this.sights.clear();
277	    this.chats.clear();
278	  }
279	}
280	
281	/** NPC.cs:79701 IsNPCValidForBestiaryKillCredit（121 不计；EoW 三段需 boss 实例旗） */
282	export function isNpcValidForBestiaryKillCredit(vanillaId: number, isBoss: boolean): boolean {
283	  if (vanillaId === 121) return false;
284	  if (vanillaId === 13 || vanillaId === 14 || vanillaId === 15) return isBoss;
285	  return true;
286	}
287	
288	// ============ 排序 id + 稀有度星（ContentSamples.NpcBestiarySortingId / NpcBestiaryRarityStars） ============
289	// 数据来源 tools/extract-bestiary.mjs → vanilla-bestiary.json：
290	//   TownNPCBestiaryPriority :4427 / NormalGoldCritterBestiaryPriority :4407 /
291	//   BossBestiaryPriority :4416（GetSortedBestiaryEntriesList orderby 三键）+
292	//   NPC.cs SetDefaults if 链 rarity 赋值 + ContentSamples.FillNpcRarities 显式覆盖(:1084-1220)
293	import bestiaryJson from './vanilla-bestiary.json';
294	
295	type BestiaryJson = typeof bestiaryJson;
296	const TOWN_PRIORITY: number[] = (bestiaryJson as BestiaryJson).townNpcBestiaryPriority;
297	const GOLD_CRITTER_PRIORITY: number[] = (bestiaryJson as BestiaryJson).normalGoldCritterBestiaryPriority;
298	const BOSS_PRIORITY: number[] = (bestiaryJson as BestiaryJson).bossBestiaryPriority;
299	const NPC_RARITY: Record<string, number> = (bestiaryJson as BestiaryJson).npcRarity;
300	const STAR_OVERRIDES: Record<string, number> = (bestiaryJson as BestiaryJson).rarityStarOverrides;
301	
302	/** NPC.isLikeATownNPC（NPC.cs:6772-6781）：453 恒真，其余走 townNPC 旗 */
303	export function isLikeATownNpc(npcId: number): boolean {
304	  return npcId === 453 || BESTIARY_TOWN_IDS.has(npcId);
305	}
306	
307	/** ContentSamples.GetNPCBestiaryRarityStarsCount（:1223-1285）1:1：
308	 *  1 + rarity（1→+1 / 2→+1.5 / 3→+2 / 4→+2.5 / 5→+3 / >5→+3.5）+ boss 0.5 +
309	 *  战力阈（>50→+1 / >150→+1.5 / >500→+2 / >1000→+2.5 / >5000→+3 / >10000→+3.5），
310	 *  钳 5 取整。FillNpcRarities 先全量算公式再显式覆盖（城镇 1-3 / Boss 4-5 段），
311	 *  覆盖优先。stats 缺表（1405 json 缺 id）按 0 计——与原版 SetDefaults 后的
312	 *  实际值可能偏差，登记待办 */
313	export function bestiaryRarityStars(npcId: number): number {
314	  const o = STAR_OVERRIDES[npcId];
315	  if (o !== undefined) return o;
316	  const v = VANILLA_NPCS[String(npcId)];
317	  const rarity = NPC_RARITY[npcId] ?? 0;
318	  const boss = VANILLA_BOSS_IDS.has(npcId);
319	  let n = 1 + rarity;
320	  if (rarity === 1) n += 1;
321	  else if (rarity === 2) n += 1.5;
322	  else if (rarity === 3) n += 2;
323	  else if (rarity === 4) n += 2.5;
324	  else if (rarity === 5) n += 3;
325	  else if (rarity > 0) n += 3.5;
326	  if (boss) n += 0.5;
327	  const power = (v?.damage ?? 0) + (v?.defense ?? 0) + Math.floor((v?.lifeMax ?? 0) / 4);
328	  if (power > 10000) n += 3.5;
329	  else if (power > 5000) n += 3;
330	  else if (power > 1000) n += 2.5;
331	  else if (power > 500) n += 2;
332	  else if (power > 150) n += 1.5;
333	  else if (power > 50) n += 1;
334	  return Math.trunc(Math.min(n, 5));
335	}
336	
337	let sortingIdCache: Map<number, number> | null = null;
338	
339	/** ContentSamples.CreateBestiarySortingIds（:1063-1073）——GetSortedBestiaryEntriesList
340	 *  （BestiaryHelper，ContentSamples.cs:745-753）九键 orderby 链的稳定全序 → 1..N 序号：
341	 *  城镇优先位 → !isLikeATownNPC → 金小动物优先位 → !CountsAsACritter → Boss 优先位 →
342	 *  群系组序 → aiStyle → 战力 → 星数（全升序；IndexOf 缺席 = MAX，Boss 缺席 = -1）。
343	 *  ⚠️ 第 6 键 GetLowestBiomeGroupIndex（:762-772）未提取——需 BestiaryDatabase 逐 NPC
344	 *  的 SpawnCondition 过滤元素表（BestiaryDatabaseNPCsPopulator 逐条注册，无静态源），
345	 *  此处以常量 0 代入：同档普通敌怪的组内相对序与原版有偏差，登记待办 */
346	export function bestiarySortingId(npcId: number): number {
347	  if (!sortingIdCache) {
348	    sortingIdCache = new Map();
349	    const townIdx = new Map(TOWN_PRIORITY.map((id, i) => [id, i]));
350	    const goldIdx = new Map(GOLD_CRITTER_PRIORITY.map((id, i) => [id, i]));
351	    const bossIdx = new Map(BOSS_PRIORITY.map((id, i) => [id, i]));
352	    const MAX = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
353	    const sortKey = (id: number): number[] => {
354	      const v = VANILLA_NPCS[String(id)];
355	      return [
356	        townIdx.get(id) ?? MAX,                 // GetBestiaryTownPriority
357	        isLikeATownNpc(id) ? 0 : 1,             // !isLikeATownNPC
358	        goldIdx.get(id) ?? MAX,                 // GetBestiaryNormalGoldCritterPriority
359	        countsAsACritter(id) ? 0 : 1,           // !CountsAsACritter
360	        bossIdx.get(id) ?? -1,                  // GetBestiaryBossPriority
361	        0,                                      // GetLowestBiomeGroupIndex（未提取，登记）
362	        v?.aiStyle ?? 0,
363	        ((v?.damage ?? 0) + (v?.defense ?? 0) + Math.floor((v?.lifeMax ?? 0) / 4)) * 4, // 战力 ×4 保整
364	        bestiaryRarityStars(id),
365	      ];
366	    };
367	    const ids = Object.keys(NPC_NAME_BY_ID)
368	      .map(Number)
369	      .filter((n) => Number.isInteger(n) && n !== 0);
370	    ids.sort((a, b) => {
371	      const ka = sortKey(a);
372	      const kb = sortKey(b);
373	      for (let i = 0; i < ka.length; i++) {
374	        if (ka[i] !== kb[i]) return ka[i] - kb[i];
375	      }
376	      return a - b;   // 全键平手：稳定序（原版 List 稳定排序保持注册序）
377	    });
378	    ids.forEach((id, i) => sortingIdCache!.set(id, i + 1));
379	  }
380	  return sortingIdCache.get(npcId) ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
381	}
382	

```

	private static int GetNPCBestiaryRarityStarsCount(NPC npc)
	{
		float num = 1f;
		num += (float)npc.rarity;
		if (npc.rarity == 1)
		{
			num += 1f;
		}
		else if (npc.rarity == 2)
		{
			num += 1.5f;
		}
		else if (npc.rarity == 3)
		{
			num += 2f;
		}
		else if (npc.rarity == 4)
		{
			num += 2.5f;
		}
		else if (npc.rarity == 5)
		{
			num += 3f;
		}
		else if (npc.rarity > 0)
		{
			num += 3.5f;
		}
		if (npc.boss)
		{
			num += 0.5f;
		}
		int num2 = npc.damage + npc.defense + npc.lifeMax / 4;
		if (num2 > 10000)
		{
			num += 3.5f;
		}
		else if (num2 > 5000)
		{
			num += 3f;
		}
		else if (num2 > 1000)
		{
			num += 2.5f;
		}
		else if (num2 > 500)
		{
			num += 2f;
		}
		else if (num2 > 150)
		{
			num += 1.5f;
		}
		else if (num2 > 50)
		{
			num += 1f;
		}
		if (num > 5f)
		{
			num = 5f;
		}
		return (int)num;
	}

151	/** 全条目行化（bestiaryEntries × tracker.unlockState × 名字解析） */
152	export function bestiaryRows(tracker: BestiaryTracker): BestiaryRow[] {
153	  const out: BestiaryRow[] = [];
154	  for (const entry of bestiaryEntries()) {
155	    let kills = 0;
156	    for (const s of entry.sources) {
157	      if (s.t === 'kill') kills = Math.max(kills, tracker.kills.get(s.credit) ?? 0);
158	    }
159	    const npcId = bestiaryRepNpcId(entry.creditId);
160	    const state = tracker.unlockState(entry);
161	    out.push({
162	      entry, npcId, kills, state,
163	      // 未解锁不显示名（原版图标 hover "???"）
164	      name: state > UnlockState.NotKnown ? npcDisplayName(npcId, entry.creditId) : '???',
165	    });
166	  }
167	  return out;
168	}

170	/** 条目显示名：正 id 走 Lang.npcName；负 netID（史莱姆/世吞段变体）Lang.npcName
171	 *  会取 abs 误命中他者，改按内部名直查；都缺回退 creditId */
172	export function npcDisplayName(npcId: number, fallback: string): string {
173	  if (npcId > 0) return Lang.npcName(npcId) ?? fallback;
174	  const internal = NPC_NAME_BY_ID[npcId];
175	  if (internal && Lang.has(`NPCName.${internal}`)) return Lang.text(`NPCName.${internal}`);
176	  return fallback;
177	}
178	
179	/** 排序（SortingSteps 各 Compare 的数据层等价；无数据步缺省，平手按 npcId 升序） */
180	export function sortBestiaryRows(rows: BestiaryRow[], key: BestiarySortKey): void {
181	  const stat = (r: BestiaryRow, f: 'lifeMax' | 'damage' | 'defense'): number =>
182	    VANILLA_NPCS[String(r.npcId)]?.[f] ?? -1;
183	  const cmp: Record<BestiarySortKey, (a: BestiaryRow, b: BestiaryRow) => number> = {
184	    // ByUnlockState：解锁档降序（已解锁在前）
185	    unlocks: (a, b) => b.state - a.state || a.npcId - b.npcId,
186	    // ByNetId 升序
187	    id: (a, b) => a.npcId - b.npcId,
188	    // ByBestiaryId：ContentSamples.NpcBestiarySortingId（九键 orderby 链序号）升序
189	    bestiaryId: (a, b) => bestiarySortingId(a.npcId) - bestiarySortingId(b.npcId),
190	    // ByRarity：NpcBestiaryRarityStars 降序
191	    rarity: (a, b) => bestiaryRarityStars(b.npcId) - bestiaryRarityStars(a.npcId) || a.npcId - b.npcId,
192	    // Alphabetical：显示名升序
193	    alpha: (a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'zh') || a.npcId - b.npcId,
194	    // ByStat 族：降序（无数据条目沉底）
195	    attack: (a, b) => stat(b, 'damage') - stat(a, 'damage') || a.npcId - b.npcId,
196	    defense: (a, b) => stat(b, 'defense') - stat(a, 'defense') || a.npcId - b.npcId,
197	    coins: (a, b) => npcValueOf(b.npcId) - npcValueOf(a.npcId) || a.npcId - b.npcId,
198	    hp: (a, b) => stat(b, 'lifeMax') - stat(a, 'lifeMax') || a.npcId - b.npcId,
199	  };
200	  rows.sort(cmp[key]);
201	}

203	/** 筛选（Filters.BySearch/ByUnlockState/ByBoss + 本仓 kind 维度） */
204	export function filterBestiaryRows(rows: BestiaryRow[], f: BestiaryFilter): BestiaryRow[] {
205	  const q = f.search.trim().toLowerCase();
206	  return rows.filter((r) => {
207	    if (f.kind !== 'all' && r.entry.kind !== f.kind) return false;
208	    if (f.boss && !isBossCredit(r.entry.creditId)) return false;
209	    if (f.unlocked === 'yes' && r.state === UnlockState.NotKnown) return false;
210	    if (f.unlocked === 'no' && r.state > UnlockState.NotKnown) return false;
211	    // BySearch：未解锁条目不提供搜索串（原版 NamePlate 隐藏）
212	    if (q && (r.state === UnlockState.NotKnown || !r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q))) return false;
213	    return true;
214	  });
215	}

	{

		if (num <= 0f)

1	// 矿车坐骑实体（原版语义 = Mount 6 "Minecart"：坐骑位锁定 + Minecart.cs 轨道跟随运动学）。
2	// 原版矿车不是独立实体——玩家 mount 后玩家 hitbox 即车（20×52，heightBoost 10），

21	  TRACK_BOOST_DELTA, PLAYER_DEFAULT_GRAVITY, RUN_SLOWDOWN,
22	  CART_TEXTURE_WIDTH,
23	} from '../world/MinecartTrack';
24	
25	/** 矿车族物品（vanilla id）→ 坐骑贴图文件（Mount.cs mounts[n].frontTexture） */
26	export const CART_TEXTURES: Record<number, string> = {
27	  2343: 'vanilla/Mount_Minecart.png',          // Minecart（mount 6）
28	  3353: 'vanilla/Mount_MinecartMech.png',      // Mechanical Cart（mount 11）
29	  4066: 'vanilla/Mount_MinecartDesert.png',    // Desert Minecart（mount 15）
30	  4426: 'vanilla/Extra_108.png',               // Bee Minecart（mount 18）
31	  4427: 'vanilla/Extra_109.png',               // Ladybug Minecart（mount 19）
32	  4428: 'vanilla/Extra_110.png',               // Pigron Minecart（mount 20）
33	  4429: 'vanilla/Extra_111.png',               // Sunflower Minecart（mount 21）
34	  4443: 'vanilla/Extra_112.png',               // Demonic Hellcart（mount 22）
35	  4450: 'vanilla/Extra_115.png',               // Shroom Minecart（mount 24）
36	};
37	export const DEFAULT_CART_TEXTURE = 'vanilla/Mount_Minecart.png';
38	
39	export class Minecart extends Entity {
40	  // 原版坐骑 hitbox：width 20、height 42 + heightBoost 10 = 52（Mount.cs mounts[6]）
41	  w = 20;
42	  h = 52;
43	  mounted = false;          // 玩家骑乘中
44	  dead = false;
45	  /** 坐骑贴图（Mount.frontTexture 文件名，atlas.ensureVImage 懒加载） */
46	  texture = DEFAULT_CART_TEXTURE;
47	  /** 升级套件已解锁（Player.UsingSuperCart → SuperCart* 常量） */
48	  superCart = false;
49	  /** 车身朝向翻转（撞弹性挡板时随输入翻；Mount._type 6 绘制翻转语义近似） */
50	  cartFlip = false;
51	  /** 车身倾角（Player.fullRotation = TrackRotation 的 atan2） */
52	  rotation = 0;
53	  /** 纵向贴轨绘制偏移（TrackRotation 返回值 = Player.gfxOffY） */
54	  gfxOffY = 0;
55	  /** 本 tick 是否在轨（Player.onTrack） */
56	  onTrackNow = false;
57	  /** 跳跃剩余 tick（Player.jump = Mount.JumpHeight；每 tick vy=-jumpSpeed） */
58	  jump = 0;
59	  jumpSpeed = CART_JUMP_SPEED;
60	  /** 加速轨道累积（Player.trackBoost：下帧并入 vx，钳 ±maxRunSpeed） */
61	  trackBoost = 0;
62	  /** 斜坡起飞动画计时（Player.cartRampTime = min(1,|vx|/RunSpeed)*20） */
63	  cartRampTime = 0;
64	  /** 轮组动画 tick（Mount runningFrame 3 帧 × delay 12） */
65	  animTick = 0;
66	  /** TrackCollision 的 lastBoost（加速轨去重：同格不重复给速） */
67	  lastBoostX = 0;
68	  lastBoostY = 0;
69	  /** 落轨音效的 fallStart（Player.fallStart：开始下落的 tile Y） */
70	  fallStart = 0;
71	
72	  private wasOnTrack = false;
73	  private prevJump = false;
74	
75	  /** 坐骑参数（SuperCart 覆盖） */
76	  get runSpeed(): number { return this.superCart ? SUPER_CART_RUN_SPEED : CART_RUN_SPEED; }
77	  get dashSpeed(): number { return this.superCart ? SUPER_CART_DASH_SPEED : CART_DASH_SPEED; }
78	  get acceleration(): number { return this.superCart ? SUPER_CART_ACCELERATION : CART_ACCELERATION; }
79	  get jumpHeight(): number { return this.superCart ? SUPER_CART_JUMP_HEIGHT : CART_JUMP_HEIGHT; }
80	
81	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
82	    // 骑乘中由 Renderer.drawPlayer 叠画（原版 frontTexture 在玩家之前层）；
83	    // 未骑乘的矿车原版不存在（坐骑 buff 结束即消失）——保留贴图绘制仅作调试残留态
84	    if (this.mounted) return;
85	    const img = r.atlas?.ensureVImage(this.texture);
86	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
87	    if (!ctx || !img) return;
88	    const fh = Math.floor(img.height / 3);
89	    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, img.width, fh,
90	      this.x + this.w / 2 - CART_TEXTURE_WIDTH / 2, this.y + this.h / 2 - fh / 2,
91	      img.width, fh);
92	  }
93	
94	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
95	    if (!this.mounted) { this.dead = true; return; }   // 坐骑语义：无骑乘即消亡
96	    const p = game.player;
97	    const st = game.world.store;
98	    const maxRun = this.runSpeed;
99	
100	    // ---- trackBoost 并入（Player.cs:19530-19544：钳 ±maxRunSpeed）----
101	    if (this.trackBoost !== 0) {
102	      this.vx += this.trackBoost;
103	      this.trackBoost = 0;
104	      if (this.vx < -maxRun) this.vx = -maxRun;
105	      else if (this.vx > maxRun) this.vx = maxRun;
106	    }
107	
108	    // ---- 水平输入（Player.cs:19546-19690 Carts 段 1:1）----
109	    // 车只在贴轨（vy==0）时响应加速/反推；runSlowdown+runAcceleration 同帧叠加
110	    const controlLeft = p.inputX < 0, controlRight = p.inputX > 0;
111	    if (controlLeft && this.vx > -maxRun) {
112	      if (this.vy === 0) {
113	        if (this.vx > RUN_SLOWDOWN) this.vx -= RUN_SLOWDOWN;
114	        this.vx -= this.acceleration;
115	      }
116	    } else if (controlRight && this.vx < maxRun) {
117	      if (this.vy === 0) {
118	        if (this.vx < -RUN_SLOWDOWN) this.vx += RUN_SLOWDOWN;
119	        this.vx += this.acceleration;
120	      }
121	    }
122	    // 朝向：仅输入方向与速度同号才转（Player.cs:19553-19561 mount.Cart 门）
123	    if (p.inputX !== 0 && Math.sign(this.vx) === p.inputX) p.facing = p.inputX;
124	
125	    // ---- 跳跃（Player.JumpMovement :20402-20418 + Mount.JumpSpeed/JumpHeight）----
126	    // 起跳需松键重按（releaseJump；autoJump 默认关），续跳按住即可（jump>0 段）
127	    this.jumpSpeed = (this.superCart ? SUPER_CART_JUMP_SPEED : CART_JUMP_SPEED);
128	    if (p.inputJump) {
129	      if (this.jump > 0) {
130	        if (this.vy === 0) this.jump = 0;
131	        else { this.vy = -this.jumpSpeed; this.jump--; }
132	      } else if (!this.prevJump && this.vy === 0 && this.onTrackNow) {
133	        // MountID.Sets.Cart 起跳：位置微抬防立即重贴轨（:20406 position.Y -= 0.001）
134	        this.y -= 0.001;
135	        this.jump = this.jumpHeight;
136	        this.vy = -this.jumpSpeed;
137	      }
138	    } else {
139	      this.jump = 0;
140	    }
141	    this.prevJump = !!p.inputJump;
142	
143	    // ---- 重力（Player.gravity = defaultGravity 0.4；在轨时 TrackCollision 清零 vy）----
144	    if (this.vy === 0) this.fallStart = Math.trunc(this.y / 16);
145	    this.vy += PLAYER_DEFAULT_GRAVITY;
146	
147	    // ---- TrackCollision（Player.cs:27795-27850 1:1）----
148	    this.wasOnTrack = this.onTrackNow;
149	    const prevVy = this.vy;
150	    const rider = {
151	      x: this.x, y: this.y, vx: this.vx, vy: this.vy,
152	      lastBoostX: this.lastBoostX, lastBoostY: this.lastBoostY,
153	    };
154	    const bits = trackCollision(
155	      st, rider, this.w, this.h,
156	      !!p.inputDown, !!p.inputUp, this.fallStart, false,
157	      {
158	        landing: (lx, ly) => {
159	          // MinecartLandingSound（DelegateMethods.Minecart.LandingSound → SoundID.Item53）+ 轮花
160	          game.playSfxFile?.('Item_53', 0.7, lx, ly);
161	          game.spawnParticles(lx, ly + this.h - 4, '#C8B08A', 6, 1.2, { life: 24 });
162	        },
163	      },
164	    );
165	    this.x = rider.x; this.y = rider.y; this.vx = rider.vx; this.vy = rider.vy;
166	    this.lastBoostX = rider.lastBoostX; this.lastBoostY = rider.lastBoostY;
167	
168	    this.onTrackNow = !!(bits & (1 << FLAG_ON_TRACK));
169	    if (this.onTrackNow) {
170	      // 倾角 + gfxOffY（Player.cs:27804-27806：位置前探一步取两轮轨面）
171	      const rot = { v: this.rotation };
172	      this.gfxOffY = trackRotation(st, this.x + this.vx, this.y + this.vy, this.w, this.h,
173	        !!p.inputDown, !!p.inputUp, rot);
174	      this.rotation = rot.v;
175	      if (this.vy === 0) this.cartRampTime = 0;   // Player.cs:26499-26501
176	      if (prevVy > PLAYER_DEFAULT_GRAVITY && this.vy === 0) this.fallStart = Math.trunc(this.y / 16);
177	    } else {
178	      this.rotation = 0;
179	      this.gfxOffY = 0;
180	      // JumpingSound 委托原版为空实现（DelegateMethods.cs:246-248）——离轨不发声
181	    }
182	    if (bits & (1 << FLAG_BOUNCY_BUMPER)) {
183	      if (controlLeft || controlRight) this.cartFlip = !this.cartFlip;
184	      if (this.vx > 0) p.facing = 1;
185	      else if (this.vx < 0) p.facing = -1;
186	      // BumperSound（DelegateMethods.Minecart.BumperSound → SoundID.Item56）
187	      game.playSfxFile?.('Item_56', 0.8, this.x, this.y);
188	    }
189	    if (bits & (1 << FLAG_USED_RAMP)) {
190	      this.cartRampTime = Math.trunc(Math.min(1, Math.abs(this.vx) / this.dashSpeed) * 20);
191	    }
192	    if (bits & (1 << FLAG_HIT_SWITCH)) {
193	      // Player.cs:27927-27931 → Minecart.HitTrackSwitch → Wiring.HitSwitch(磁石位)
194	      const mx = Math.trunc((this.x + this.w / 2 - CART_TEXTURE_WIDTH / 2 + 25) / 16);
195	      const my = Math.trunc((this.y + this.h / 2 + 26) / 16);
196	      game.hitTrackSwitch?.(mx, my);
197	    }
198	    if (bits & (1 << FLAG_BOOST_LEFT)) this.trackBoost -= TRACK_BOOST_DELTA;
199	    if (bits & (1 << FLAG_BOOST_RIGHT)) this.trackBoost += TRACK_BOOST_DELTA;
200	
201	    // ---- 位置积分（原版在 TrackCollision 后由常规碰撞段 position += velocity 完成：
202	    // TrackCollision 只改速度 + 贴轨 Y 快照。轨道非实心——横向撞实心墙即停回退）----
203	    this.x += this.vx;
204	    this.y += this.vy;
205	    if (this.vx !== 0) {
206	      const headX = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2 + Math.sign(this.vx) * this.w / 2) / 16);
207	      const feetY = Math.floor((this.y + this.h - 4) / 16);
208	      if (st.inBounds(headX, feetY) && st.isSolid(headX, feetY)) {
209	        this.x -= this.vx;
210	        this.vx = 0;
211	      }
212	    }
213	
214	    if (Math.abs(this.vx) > 0.1) this.animTick++;
215	    else this.animTick = 0;
216	
217	    // ---- 玩家坐骑位回写（原版玩家 hitbox 即车；此处锁玩家到车位）----
218	    p.x = this.x;
219	    p.y = this.y;
220	    p.vx = this.vx;
221	    p.vy = 0;
222	    p.onGround = this.onTrackNow;
223	    p.fallStartY = null;   // 车上不计摔伤（原版 mount fallStart 语义）
224	
225	    // 完全脱轨且离轨远处（误用兜底）：无轨重力自然下落由 TileCollision 缺失 → 消亡防穿地
226	    if (!this.onTrackNow && this.vy > 0 && !onTrack(st, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h)
227	      && this.y > game.world.store.h * 16 - 64) {
228	      this.dead = true;
229	      this.mounted = false;
230	    }
231	  }
232	
233	  /** 轮组动画帧（Mount runningFrameCount 3 / runningFrameDelay 12；静止 standingFrame 0） */
234	  get animFrame(): number {
235	    return Math.abs(this.vx) > 0.1 ? Math.floor(this.animTick / 12) % 3 : 0;
236	  }
237	}

98	function shimmerWorld(): World {

100	  const st = w.store;

102	    st.setTile(x, 40, 1);               // 池底

104	  }

106	  w.lavaLine = 50;
107	  return w;
108	}

The shimmer test is very substantive. One weak spot at line 165-168: `if (inPool) { ... }` — conditional assertion (if inPool is null, the test passes trivially). But this is documented as "微光柱内不放人" — the assertion only runs if a spot was found. If findShimmerFreeSpot returned null, the test would pass without verifying anything. That's a conditional-assertion soft spot but the second part of the test does assert non-null for the pool-outside case. Minor.

Now let's check the bestiary-ui test and projectile-reflect test.

18	
19	/** sheet id → 内部 tile id（与 WorldEvolution.SID 同构，模块私有故测试内重建） */
20	const SID = new Map<number, number>();
21	for (let id = 0; id < TILE_DEFS.length; id++) {
22	  const sh = TILE_DEFS[id]?.vanilla?.sheet;
23	  if (typeof sh === 'number' && !SID.has(sh)) SID.set(sh, id);
24	}
25	const S = (sheet: number): number => SID.get(sheet) ?? -1;
26	
27	/** png 尺寸（IHDR：宽高各 4 字节大端，偏移 16/20） */
28	function pngSize(buf: Buffer): [number, number] {
29	  return [buf.readUInt32BE(16), buf.readUInt32BE(20)];
30	}
31	
32	function evoAt(jAbove: boolean) {
33	  const w = new World(24, 24, 7, 'evo');
34	  const host: EvolutionHost = {
35	    store: w.store, worldSurface: 12, rockLevel: 18,
36	    flags: w.flags, crimson: false, isDay: true, raining: false,
37	    maxRaining: 0, windSpeedCurrent: 0, playerTile: () => null,
38	  };
39	  return { w, st: w.store, evo: new WorldEvolution(host), j: jAbove ? 6 : 20 };
40	}
41	
42	describe('Convert case 11 净化（WorldGen.cs:55792-55902）', () => {
43	  it.each([
44	    [25, 1],    // 黑檀石 → 石
45	    [23, 2],    // 腐化草 → 草
46	    [112, 53],  // 黑檀沙 → 沙
47	    [117, 1],   // 珍珠石（神圣）也还原为石——净化对邪恶/神圣双向生效
48	    [109, 2],   // 神圣草 → 草
49	    [400, 396], // 腐化沙岩 → 沙岩
50	    [661, 60],  // 腐化丛林草 → 丛林草
51	  ])('tile sheet %i → %i', (from, to) => {
52	    const { st, evo, j } = evoAt(false);
53	    st.setTileSilent(4, j, S(from));
54	    evo.convert(4, j, 11);
55	    expect(TILE_DEFS[st.type[st.idx(4, j)]]?.vanilla?.sheet).toBe(to);
56	  });
57	
58	  it('墙：腐化草墙 69（地表）→ 原版草墙 63/65 档；石族墙 → 349', () => {
59	    const above = evoAt(true);
60	    above.st.setWallSilent(4, above.j, 69);
61	    const spy1 = vi.spyOn(Math, 'random').mockReturnValue(0); // 9/10 档 → 63
62	    above.evo.convert(4, above.j, 11);
63	    spy1.mockRestore();
64	    expect(above.st.wall[above.st.idx(4, above.j)]).toBe(63);
65	
66	    const below = evoAt(false);
67	    below.st.setWallSilent(4, below.j, 69);
68	    below.evo.convert(4, below.j, 11);
69	    expect(below.st.wall[below.st.idx(4, below.j)]).toBe(64); // 地下档 64
70	
71	    const stone = evoAt(false);
72	    stone.st.setWallSilent(6, stone.j, 83); // 猩红石墙 ∈ Stone 集 → 349
73	    stone.evo.convert(6, stone.j, 11);
74	    expect(stone.st.wall[stone.st.idx(6, stone.j)]).toBe(349);
75	  });
76	});

So the average of the bilinear interpolation ≠ average of the 4 corner values in general (equality holds only for bilinear functions; remap's clamping breaks it). The comment claims "数值等价（全网格采样验证平均差 0.0000）" — that claim is mathematically dubious but they say they verified empirically over the grid. Actually for bilinear interpolation of corner values f(0,0),f(1,0),f(0,1),f(1,1), the area average IS exactly the mean of the 4 corners. But the original interpolates the underlying function's corner VALUES (each corner computed by the full GetShimmerGlitterOpacity function) then bilinearly interpolates those values → the area average of that interpolation = mean of 4 corner values exactly (bilinear property). Yes! Because the vertex color IS the corner value, and bilinear interpolation's area average equals the arithmetic mean of the 4 corners. So it IS exactly equivalent. ✓ Good reasoning, no issue.

Now, also note: in vanilla the sparkle alpha gate uses `ptr2->Opacity` (line 733: SetShimmerVertexColors_Sparkle(ref vertices, ptr2->Opacity, ...)) NOT `val` (which is min(1, opacity*(bg?1:0.75))). The port comment says "sparkle 不乘前景 0.75/背景 1 系数" and uses `opacity[i]`. ✓ Correct — matches ptr2->Opacity.

Wait, but there's a subtlety: in the port, the sparkle draw uses `ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, opacity[i] * ga)`. And the base uses `Math.min(1, opacity[i] * (isBackground ? 1 : 0.75))`. ✓ Consistent with val vs ptr2->Opacity.

One more: the base tint pass — vanilla multiplies the texture by vertex color = white × opacity × baseColor. So the final pixel = texel × opacity × baseColor (modulate, RGB). The port draws the base texture at globalAlpha=min(1, opacity*(bg?1:0.75)) then multiplies baseColor with globalAlpha=1 ('multiply' composite). But vanilla multiplies by the FULL opacity (val includes 0.75 for foreground), i.e., the alpha channel too — the vertex color alpha = opacity × baseColor.A where baseColor.A=1 → alpha = opacity. So texel alpha × opacity. The port's drawImage with globalAlpha=opacity*(0.75 or 1) × then multiply composite... In Canvas 'multiply', source alpha is also considered: multiply composite computes Cs*Cb with alpha blending. The result: dst = src*dst*(...) blended. Actually 'multiply' with globalAlpha=1: result = (1-αs)*dst + αs*(src*dst) per channel. Since the base texture was already drawn, applying multiply with a full-opacity colored rect gives texel*tint (per channel) but also affects alpha via standard Porter-Duff. This approximates the modulate. It's a documented Canvas2D-best-approximation. Fine.

Hmm wait — there IS a potential issue with the base tint: the port's applyShimmerBaseTint forces globalAlpha = 1 and uses 'multiply'. In the foreground pass, vanilla's vertex color alpha = opacity*0.75, so texels with partial alpha get... Actually in vanilla the vertex color multiplies RGBA: final color = texel.RGBA × (opacity × baseColor.RGBA). baseColor.A = 1 always (Vector4 lerp with A=1 endpoints). So alpha factor = opacity (val). RGB factor = opacity × baseColor.RGB. So the effective premultiplied = opacity² × baseColor × texelRGB. The port: draws at alpha = opacity (source-over), then multiply tint at alpha 1. Multiply in canvas: result_rgb = src_rgb*dst_rgb (for opaque src). So texel_rgb × baseColor × (opacity applied at draw time). The alpha: drawImage alpha = texel_a × opacity. So rgb = texel_rgb × baseColor × ... but the initial draw darkens rgb? No — initial draw with source-over at globalAlpha doesn't darken RGB, just alpha. Then multiply multiplies rgb by baseColor. So rgb = texel_rgb × baseColor, alpha = texel_a × opacity. Vanilla: rgb = texel_rgb × opacity × baseColor, alpha = texel_a × opacity. For partially transparent texels the port's rgb is brighter relative to alpha (non-premultiplied difference), but for fully opaque water texels rgb matches vanilla. Minor, not worth flagging given it's the documented "Canvas2D 最优可达".

Now let me quickly verify the drops dir decraft integration exists (already saw Shimmer.ts). Check NpcDrops for anything shimmer-related — the caller's scope includes src/drops/. Let me do a fast scan of NpcDrops.ts for the new stuff (bound chain? lifesteal? no). Probably decraft is in ItemDrop/Game. Fine.

Let me also confirm: is there any place where Enemy shimmerT decay `-1` should be `-0.001*100 = 0.1`? shimmerT is in units of 0.01 (port comment: "≈shimmerTransparency ×100"). Vanilla decay 0.001/t → in port units 0.1/t (if not immune) or 1.5/t (if immune, but immune never gets shimmering>0). justHit → 10 units. Port uses 1 unit = 0.01 = 10× too fast vs vanilla 0.001. So decay is 10× faster than vanilla. That's an undocumented deviation. Minor (visual only, both enemy and critter). I'll list under ⚠️.

Actually wait, let me double check: NPC.cs:92484-92500: `else if (shimmerTransparency > 0f) { if (justHit) shimmerTransparency -= 0.1f; if (buffImmune[353]) shimmerTransparency -= 0.015f; else shimmerTransparency -= 0.001f; ... }`. Yes. So decay 0.001/t for normal NPCs. Port: shimmerT -= 1 → in transparency units 0.01. 10× faster. Flag as ⚠️ minor (visual timing only, and the port explicitly says "视觉侧未接（GAP），此处只保留转化触发计时" — the shimmerT is only used for the trigger, not visuals, so the decay rate doesn't matter functionally except re-entry timing). Indeed shimmerT only gates the >90 trigger; a faster decay just means a slightly shorter "memory" after leaving the pool. Very low impact. I'll mention briefly.

Let me also double-check the Enemy shimmer trigger threshold: `shimmerT > 90` → vanilla shimmerTransparency > 0.9 with +0.01/t starting from 0 → triggers at t=91 (0.91). Port: starts 0, +1/t, triggers when > 90 → t=91. ✓ Exact.
